2026-10-14T17:21:37.6974186Z ##[debug]script=#!/bin/bash
set -o errexit
set -o pipefail
set -x

shopt -s expand_aliases

if [[ -n "$SITE" && "okd4_nprd" =~ "ocp" ]]
then
  app="sijur-middleware-backend-des"

  arquivo="/usr/local/bin/oc-v4.***3"
  if [ -e "$arquivo" ]; then 
    alias oc="$arquivo"
  fi
elif [[ -n "$SITE" && "$SITE" =~ (okd4|openshift) ]];
then
app="sijur-middleware-backend-des"
else
  app="sijur-middleware-backend-des-esteiras"
fi

oc version

last_pod=$(oc get pod -l name="$app" -n sijur-des -o jsonpath='{range .items[*]}{.metadata.name}{"\n"}' --sort-by=.metadata.creationTimestamp  | tac | grep -v '^$' | head -n***)

echo "Logs do POD: $last_pod"
oc logs $last_pod -c "$app" -n sijur-des
